id=\"offerings_update_form_24745_offerings_update_body\"\u003e\n\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e(THIS UPDATE WAS ALSO DELIVERED BY EMAIL. PLEASE CHECK SPAM FOLDERS AND MARK IT AS SAFE SO THAT YOU DO NOT MISS FUTURE IMPORTANT UPDATES ABOUT YOUR INVESTMENT)\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eDear Investor,\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eI hope you're doing well. We've got some important updates that feel like catalysts for Applaudable.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eProduct Evolution\u003c/strong\u003e\u003cbr\u003eI'll start with what I'm most excited about: the linchpin of all other developments. Although it's challenging to capture in a single paragraph, we're thrilled about the upcoming version of Applaudable. We've synthesized the best elements from 39 previous versions into a cohesive experience that we believe clearly conveys our unique value proposition. In addition to making substantial improvements to how (and what kinds of) experiences can be Applauded, we've engineered unique features like \"Circles\" and experience-driven post permanency for both immediate utility and long-term engagement. We believe this upcoming release not only facilitates a more positive and authentic experience that today's social media norms, but also fills gaps in communication apps like WhatsApp. Furthermore, with our initial network launches centering around school communities, we've also emphasized functionality and privacy for younger user demographics.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eFirst Networks Launching in January\u003c/strong\u003e\u003cbr\u003eOur first set of networks launching in January are not just a debut; they validate a network launch model that maximizes user value based on network density. They also provide a repeatable template with a level of scalability that can't be overemphasized. As Andrew Chen of Andreessen Horowitz—known for substantial investments in leading social media companies—said, \"When a startup shows a small curve, you should probably try to invest.\" We have made substantial investments in product and strategy to achieve the exact curve to which he was referring, with our upcoming network launches, and we aim to replicate this across many other networks worldwide.\u0026nbsp;\u003cbr\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eManagement \u0026amp; Leadership\u003c/strong\u003e\u003cbr\u003eWe're onboarding a new CTO, a tech industry veteran who has led key development teams at both Microsoft and Meta. An official press release and a detailed announcement are forthcoming. His experience is a significant asset as we push our technological and product development boundaries.\u003cbr\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eSteve Davis\u003c/strong\u003e\u003cbr\u003eSteve Davis has also formally joined our Board of Advisors. He brings a unique and deep history of experience in scaling businesses, having been involved in the founding and executive teams of GSI Commerce (sold to eBay for $2.5 billion), Rue Gilt Groupe, and most recently Fanatics, which is currently valued at $31 billion. In addition to joining our Board of Advisors, Steve has made Applaudable his first outside venture investment, further solidifying his belief and support in the company.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eDeparture\u003c/strong\u003e\u003cbr\u003eWe've parted ways with Laurence Levi, enabling us to reallocate resources and focus on capitalization strategies that are more aligned with the company's current needs and future goals. This shift allows both Laurence and us to pursue paths that best serve our respective interests. We value Laurence's time with the company and wish him well in his future endeavors.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eCapital Raise Update\u003c/strong\u003e\u003cbr\u003eAfter the significant progress we've made since suspending our public capital raise last month, for the time being, we've limited deal-access to private investors who have expressed interest in increasing their participation. Given our strong belief in the upcoming product release and the scalable network launch set for January, we've decided against relaunching a public offering for now. As we near a successful network launch—which has the potential to catapult growth—we believe institutional investment is becoming the most strategic fit for our future capital needs.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eYour continued support and confidence are greatly appreciated and vital for driving these pivotal initiatives forward. We remain committed to delivering exceptional value for you and all stakeholders involved and have never felt better about our direction.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eIf you have any questions or would like to discuss these updates further, feel free to reach out directly.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eBest regards,\u003cbr\u003eAndrew Christodoulides\u003cbr\u003eFounder \u0026amp; CEO, people are hungry for a more meaningful, more authentic social media platform.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eLearn more about our current raise\u0026nbsp;\u003ca href=\"https://welivv.us14.list-manage.com/track/click?u=551376191594ddd99960cf8c0\u0026amp;id=f35b533b2a\u0026amp;e=2974db5a17\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003ehere\u003c/a\u003e. \u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eThat authenticity is built into our DNA.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eBut we aren’t stopping there. \u0026nbsp;We’re excited to give you a sneak peek into some key updates coming in our next release.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003col\u003e\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eVelvet Rope Access:\u003c/strong\u003e Our initial public release will include a unique \"velvet rope\" feature. Users will need an invitation to gain access, ensuring we can maintain optimal network density and control our growth trajectory during the early stages.\u003c/li\u003e\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eHighlights:\u003c/strong\u003e Upon publishing a post, users will have the option to enhance it by adding highlights. These highlights serve as a way for users to applaud specific parts of their original post. Imagine pointing out the standout moments of a trip as they unfold.\u003c/li\u003e\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eVideo Integration:\u003c/strong\u003e Users can now incorporate videos into their posts. A single post can feature up to 10 photos and/or videos. Even better? Each highlight within the post can contain its own set of 10.\u003c/li\u003e\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eDisappearing Posts:\u003c/strong\u003e\u0026nbsp; Posts will automatically vanish after 24 hours. However, users can immortalize their most notable experiences by adding requisite details to the original post, making the shared experience replicable for others. While vanished posts leave the public eye, they'll stay in the user's private diary, ready to be made public again at the user's discretion.\u003c/li\u003e\u003c/ol\u003e\u003cp\u003eIn addition to these new features, we're constantly refining the platform, especially our \"Real Life Stories\" segment. Our vision remains clear: to position Applaudable as the premier platform for capturing and sharing the world's most memorable experiences. We’re looking forward to you being a part of what we believe will be the future of social media and we thank you for the continued support in our vision.\u003cbr\u003e\u003cbr\u003eWith id=\"offerings_update_form_8466_offerings_update_body\"\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eDear Investor,\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eWe've been overwhelmed by all the support we've received during the campaign and even since it ended. The first phase of the Applaudable Series A closed on Republic at $1.88M, making it one of few deals to have successfully closed two consecutive offerings on the Republic platform. We're very grateful to everyone who invested! And we're also thankful to our investors, who are offering support through their personal areas of expertise and introductions to others in their respective networks. We thank you all and will reply to each of you individually as quickly as possible.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eFor today's update, I wanted to express excitement about being able to focus on the work again. We're pressing pause on raising capital for now so that we can focus on achieving the objectives you've heard throughout our campaign. We are starting with our number one priority: release the first public version of Applaudable. And to this end, I want to announce that we have officially finalized the expansion of our product development team to include ten incredible designers, engineers, and strategists who were previously at the core of the social media app Triller (valued at $5 billion, December 2021). We can't wait to bring Applaudable into the world, and we couldn't be more excited about the product team with who we'll be doing it. \u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eWe will also remain maniacally focused on keeping the expansion of users and marketplace revenue on track with projections.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eWe believe that hitting our target milestones may position us to open up the next phase of the offering at a premium to the $35 million pre-money valuation that was just closed.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eI couldn't be happier to have you along for the ride. Thank you, and stay tuned.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eWith gratitude,\u003cbr\u003eAndrew Christodoulides\u003cbr\u003eFounder \u0026amp; id=\"offerings_update_form_7969_offerings_update_body\"\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eAs this stage of our Series A closes, I wanted to first express excitement and gratitude to each of you. We have deep conviction in what we’re building, and the significance of how much our user growth has validated market demand can’t be overstated. None of this would be possible without each of you. I thank you for your belief and support. Many of you are colleagues, family, friends, and partners. Many others I don’t know personally. I feel a deep sense of obligation to each of you, and I am committed to making your investments extraordinarily successful.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eWe are wrapping up the offering on Republic today and will be sharing details on what’s next for the company, including, of course, the public release of Applaudable. For now, I wanted to share some very exciting news briefly. We have reached a verbal agreement to expand our product team to include 8-10 engineers, designers, and product strategists, who led key development for Triller, which achieved a $5 billion valuation. We hope to have this finalized in the coming days. This product team expansion and the immense experience they bring will help ensure we deliver the type of product our tens of thousands of users expect. With the level of user growth we’ve already experienced, delivering a great user experience, I believe, is the single most important thing we can do to drive enterprise value. And you’ve seen the comps to Clubhouse and others.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eStay tuned.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eWith gratitude,\u003cbr\u003eAndrew Christodoulides\u003cbr\u003eFounder id=\"offerings_update_form_7909_offerings_update_body\"\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eAs we close in on the final days of our raise, I want to share a personal note about the mission that drives me and the entire Applaudable team to be totally relentless, every day, about executing on our vision.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eOur vision is to organize the world's greatest experiences and make them accessible and repeatable by others. This means every experience that has ever meant something to someone - whether in the impact it may have had in shaping someone's life, or something that someone has simply enjoyed or appreciated somehow.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eOnce again, awareness and appreciation of what has meaning is actually what gives it meaning. Applaudable aims to improve our collective awareness and recognition of experiences that warrant appreciation. We will enable sharing of those experiences in a way that others can repeat.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eThe idea that we can capture and organize any great or meaningful experience anyone has ever had in a way that will both improve the experience as well as enable others to repeat the experience is something I view as incredibly inspiring. The potential impact can't be overstated.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eWe are also profoundly inspired by the vision of what that future will look like. We're humbled to have attracted more than 5,000 investors along the way, and to have more user registrations at our stage of development than Instagram or Clubhouse.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eAnd we are maniacally driven to do everything possible within our capability, to pull every lever at our disposal, to bring that future to life, and validate the extraordinary confidence of our investors, our partners and our employees.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eWe have less than 5 days left in the current public offering at http://republic.com/applaudable. We don't know when we will reopen the round and if it will be available to the public, but we believe it will be at a higher valuation.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eWhile we continue to grow users, delivering a product people are regularly using may enable us to open the next round at a significantly higher valuation. We're so grateful to have you along with us, and we look forward to achieving something truly special together.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eWith gratitude,\u003cbr\u003eAndrew You've now attracted more than 5,000 investors. How does that feel?\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eA. It's humbling. It really is. And we couldn't be more excited about having them on this incredible journey with us. Many of these investors are friends and family, but the vast majority of them, I don't know. I'm committed to making all of their investments extraordinarily successful.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eQ. To what do you credit the success of this offering.\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eA. We're building something truly differentiated with immense potential. We've also validated it through user registrations beyond what the leading platforms in history have had at this stage. We get that not everyone has the ability to see that. But thousands of people have, and again, we're committed to making sure they are right in getting behind what we're doing. \u0026nbsp;\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eQ. What is it that you are most excited about?\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eA. We are going to organize the world's greatest experiences and make them accessible and repeatable by others.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eThis is my 3rd startup. I have genuinely never felt excitement or conviction like this. This, to me, is more than just a business. I believe that awareness and appreciation of what has meaning is actually what gives it meaning. Most of us are not even aware of all of the great things we've experienced throughout our lives. That's the equivalent of not having experienced those things at all. I genuinely believe Applaudable can change that. The potential impact on users is immense, and the scalability of the platform and business can't be overstated. The fact that tens of thousands of users have flocked to it without any advertising or paid media is a testament to the market feeling the same.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eTo say I'm excited would be an understatement. But, one thing I am not is surprised.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eQ. What are your current priorities?\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eA. I'm focused on product. As I've said before, at this stage, especially given our user growth, nothing is more important than delivering a live product that meets the expectations of the vast amount of users that have registered. And I believe doing this may have a significant impact on the value of the company (and, therefore, on the value of investments).\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eWe have one week left in the current public offering at \u003ca href=\"http://republic.com/applaudable\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003erepublic.com/applaudable\u003c/a\u003e. We don't know when we will reopen the round and if it will be available to the public, but we believe it will be at a higher valuation. While we continue to grow users, delivering a product people are regularly using may enable us to open the next round at a significantly higher valuation.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e—\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cem\u003e\u003cem\u003eThis view is from a third party. \u0026nbsp;There may be other factors such as various risks discussed or name=\"offerings_update[body]\" id=\"offerings_update_form_7866_offerings_update_body\"\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eI’d like to share some consequential news related to the Applaudable investment offering. Our Board of directors has confirmed our intention to end our capital raise as scheduled in 11 days. On June 4, 2022, we will end access through Republic, and the investment will no longer be available to the public.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eIn order to achieve our product development and user growth objectives, we are targeting $2mm and will focus on filling any remaining amount through larger investors offline. \u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eWe believe that $2mm will enable us to release the web version of Applaudable and acquire 500k total users. We are targeting 50% regular monthly activity among these users, and this is something we believe we will be well-positioned to achieve.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eWhile there are other moving parts, we expect that achieving our product release and user growth/activity objectives would position us to raise a Series B at a significant premium to the current $35mm valuation. While we may make this phase of the offering public, this is not yet decided. We are also considering and having early discussions with institutional investors who may be a better fit for our capital needs.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eThe offering is live and available at the $35mm valuation. To learn more, visit \u003ca href=\"//republic.com/Applaudable\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003erepublic.com/Applaudable\u003c/a\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eWith gratitude,\u003cbr\u003eAndrew id=\"offerings_update_form_7774_offerings_update_body\"\u003e\n\u003ch2\u003eWe have big news.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/h2\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eMillerKnoll is launching a major digital partnership with Moro, Applaudable's home furnishings ecommerce retailer.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/strong\u003e\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eAs you know, Applaudable's business model is to generate purchase transactions from Applaudable user posts. \u0026nbsp;Some of those purchases will be fulfilled by third party ecommerce sites owned by \u0026nbsp;affiliates; and some will be fulfilled by ecommerce sites we own, like Moro. \u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eAnd this is big news for Moro.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eMiller Knoll is significantly reducing the retailers it sells into – but they just selected Moro as their only new digital retail partner across their extraordinary portfolio of home furnishings brands. \u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eThere are \u003cstrong\u003efour reasons\u003c/strong\u003e why this is such a big deal.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e1. \u0026nbsp;Validating Our Strategy.\u003c/strong\u003e\u0026nbsp; When Herman Miller acquired Knoll last year, the newly-formed MillerKnoll instantly became the preeminent global leader in modern design, with \u003cstrong\u003e11,000 employees, 19 brands sold in more than 100 countries, and a $2.4B market cap.\u003c/strong\u003e\u0026nbsp; Their iconic brands include the Herman Miller Aeron office chair, the legendary Eames chair and many more. \u0026nbsp;They are a global thought leader in the design business. Their partnership with us is a \u003cstrong\u003etremendous validator of Moro’s highly-differentiated visual merchandising strategy, and a tremendous validator of Applaudable’s strategy of building a superior social media platform\u0026nbsp;\u003c/strong\u003ethat fosters the sharing of life’s greatest experiences, with the seamless ability to transact from posts – which MillerKnoll cited as instrumental in their decision to partner with us.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e\u0026nbsp;2. Opening Doors for Other Brands.\u003c/strong\u003e\u0026nbsp; MillerKnoll has extraordinary influence in the design community. \u0026nbsp;The definitive global trade show in the furnishings and design business, Salon del Mobile in Milan, Italy, is less than a month away. \u0026nbsp;With MillerKnoll’s validation, we expect to have a powerful \u003cstrong\u003ewind at our back in discussions in Milan about acquiring other brands\u003c/strong\u003e to sell through Moro. \u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e\u0026nbsp;3. Opening Doors for Other Ecommerce Categories.\u003c/strong\u003e\u0026nbsp; With Moro’s success, we are looking at additional ecommerce categories where we think our groundbreaking visual merchandising approach will enable us to deliver a fundamentally better solution to consumers. \u0026nbsp;We’ll update you with more specifics on that soon – but our success in attracting the global leader in design to Moro, gives us \u003cstrong\u003eenormous confidence about our ability to attract similar “category killer” brands\u0026nbsp;\u003c/strong\u003ein other high touch categories such as jewelry, apparel and footwear that we are considering for our next owned ecommerce sites.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e\u0026nbsp;4. \u0026nbsp;Financial Impact for Moro.\u003c/strong\u003e\u0026nbsp; Finally, we will continue to be conservative in our financial forecasting. \u0026nbsp;We aren’t going to increase our revenue target for Moro based on this news – yet. \u0026nbsp;But bringing on the biggest brand in the space should absolutely \u003cstrong\u003emove the needle for us financially\u003c/strong\u003e, in a very meaningful way.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eMore to come. \u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eWith gratitude,\u003cbr\u003eAndrew Christodoulides\u003cbr\u003eFounder \u0026amp; The significance of converting the users who have already registered to active users of Applaudable cannot be overstated. And so, delivering a product they love is the most important thing I can do for the company and all of its stakeholders at this point.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eOur goal is to ensure at least 50% of our registered users actively use the live platform. I believe that achieving our MAU targets will enable us to increase our valuation significantly. And continuing to rapidly grow users at that time can drive that valuation even higher. \u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eWe’re working tirelessly to make this happen. offering.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eIt’s gratifying to bring you this type of news on our progress, and we couldn’t be more excited about what’s That's already more than Clubhouse achieved in its first six months. But we now think we will double – \u003cem\u003eor even triple\u003c/em\u003e – that number.\u003c/div\u003e\u003cdiv\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\u003cdiv\u003eEcommerce remains a core business for us. \u0026nbsp;Moro, our first e-commerce marketplace in the home furnishing category, is showing excellent traction, with a $4mm revenue run rate. And we continue to look closely at launching ecommerce marketplaces in other verticals that, like home furnishings, are poorly merchandised and will benefit from the enhanced decision support that we can provide through Applaudable. Moro and each of our additional marketplaces will be integrated into Applaudable, enabling the purchasability of products directly from the user-generated photos in which they appear. But we will are approaching escape velocity with Applaudable. \u0026nbsp;And that’s where our focus will be.\u003c/div\u003e\u003cdiv\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\u003cdiv\u003eWe’re thrilled time: Our Applaudable social media platform is catching fire. \u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eEvery day another story emerges about how \u003ca href=\"https://www.wsj.com/articles/facebook-knows-instagram-is-toxic-for-teen-girls-company-documents-show-11631620739\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003etoxic\u003c/a\u003e social media has become. \u0026nbsp;Instagram feeds are inundated with superficial posts, and sponsored content from advertisers. \u0026nbsp;And the utility of social media is lousy too: It’s nearly impossible to purchase items featured in posts, because the posts lack the needed data to transact. \u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eThat’s why some observers are saying Instagram is \u003ca href=\"https://mashable.com/article/instagram-flop-era\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003edying\u003c/a\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eOur approach with Applaudable is just better. \u0026nbsp;It’s about appreciating life’s greatest experiences, not about toxic “living my best life” posts. \u0026nbsp;It’s about using our Social Search approach to find amazing, authentic content from people whose interests you share - not from advertisers. \u0026nbsp;And it’s about being able to seamlessly purchase the items you see in users’ posts - right on the Applaudable platform.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eWe think that's why our user registration numbers continue to grow explosively. As I mentioned last week, we originally hoped we would have a many as 100,000 users registered for our beta launch in April. That's already more than Clubhouse achieved in its first six months. But we now think we will double or even triple that number. \u0026nbsp;Accordingly, we're continuing to focus resources on Applaudable.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eMoro, our first e-commerce marketplace in the home furnishing category, is showing excellent traction as well, with a $4mm revenue run rate. And we continue to look closely at launching ecommerce marketplaces in other verticals that, like home furnishings, are poorly merchandised and will benefit from the enhanced decision support that we can provide through Applaudable. Ecommerce will remain a core business for us. Moro and each of our additional marketplaces will be integrated into Applaudable, enabling the purchasability of products directly from the user-generated photos in which they appear.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eWith the Applaudable momentum so strong, going forward, you'll see more emphasis in our strategy centering around it.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eWe’re class=\"c-input-group\"\u003e\u003clabel class=\"c-label\" for=\"offerings_update_form_6102_offerings_update_headline\"\u003eTitle\u003c/label\u003e\u003cinput required=\"required\" class=\"c-input class='js-headline_error u-colorRed s-marginPullTop0_5 s-marginBottom1 s-marginLeft0_5 u-hidden'\u003e\nEntry must be under 140 characters.\n\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cdiv class='offerings-updates-shared-form__body-wrapper'\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"c-input-group\"\u003e\u003ctextarea class=\"c-input--textarea c-input c-input--required js-offering_update_body\" required=\"required\" data-show-video-autoplay-button=\"false\" data-file-formats=\"application/msword application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document application/pdf application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.presentationml.presentation\" aria-required=\"true\" placeholder=\"Description\" name=\"offerings_update[body]\" id=\"offerings_update_form_6102_offerings_update_body\"\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eMany of you are asking questions surrounding “social search” and whether our vision for Applaudable is more of a Social Media or an Online Search platform. I thought I’d try to combine answers to all related questions here for all to see. I strongly recommend reviewing this for a better understanding of the process and its significance.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eTo answer questions about utility first... The way I see it, the greatest potential for Applaudable is that it becomes a medium for intent-based search for a broad range of products, places, brands, and activities. We believe we are truly on a path to revolutionizing online search by having search results represented entirely by authentic, visual user general experiences, rather than by advertiser or promotional content. That said, the path to this is through a social network that allows users to authentically and objectively share their greatest experiences, and discover (through a feed) those of others based on relevant interests. This is exactly how we started with Moro, focusing on the interiors and home furnishings categories, where we quickly achieved critical mass. As critical mass is now achieved within additional individual categories, feed tags will be shown to enable filtering within feeds. This would be the final stage before ultimately enabling search functionality when the content and data support likely relevant results.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eBased on extensive testing, I'm very confident in our ability to grow users, and so most of our energy and resources focus on the value these users will both produce for and get from the platform. From a sharing standpoint, the three tenets for me are:\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eAwareness\u003c/strong\u003e - For an active platform user, the process of starting to consciously even think about the experiences they're lucky to have, is a benefit in and of itself, and a contrast to how most people operate/appreciate today. \u0026nbsp;You may have heard me say in one of the videos: \"Awareness and appreciation of what has meaning is actually what gives it meaning\" - This is obviously true for all of that we're lucky to have.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eRecognition\u003c/strong\u003e - the act of sharing great experiences improves the experience, hence is why platforms like Instagram are successful.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eAltruism\u003c/strong\u003e - In addition to sharing offering similar benefits to sharing on IG or anywhere else, I believe there is additional value in being able to share experiences in a way that makes the same experience possible for others. This is something we'll report against so users understand the impact their posts have on others (how many people read and loved xyz book because of my Applauding it).\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eThese are the benefits of sharing. And maximizing this is our top priority beyond getting users to register. From a consumption standpoint, as I mentioned, initially through a discovery feed, users will be shown the most relevant experiences of other users, until we reach critical mass to begin allowing some more control of what they see using filters and ultimately search functionality.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eRegarding the business model, Affiliate which makes feed/search results accessible on-platform (similar to how purchases of tagged/shoppable items work on IG) is what I believe is right, both in the early stages, but also in a fully actualized version of the platform. I believe it is the greatest value add for users and more scalable than an e-comm play that would warrant our involvement with the user/consumer beyond the point of sale.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eWe appreciate the questions - please keep them coming.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eVery Best, Andrew Christodoulides, Founder \u0026amp; CEO\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eapplaudable.com/andrew\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eYou can find details on the early allocation of the Series A offering \u003ca href=\"http://republic.co/moro-applaudable\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003ehere\u003c/a\u003e.\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cem\u003eImportant Disclaimer: \u003c/em\u003e\u003cem\u003eAn offering statement pursuant to Regulation A relating to these securities has been filed with the Securities and Exchange Commission. Information contained in this Preliminary Offering Circular is subject to completion or amendment. These securities may not be sold nor may offers to buy be accepted before the offering statement filed with the Commission is qualified. This Preliminary Offering Circular shall not constitute an offer to sell or the solicitation of an offer to buy nor may there be any sales of these securities in any state in which such offer, solicitation or sale would be unlawful before registration or qualification under the laws of any such state. We may elect to satisfy our obligation to deliver a Final Offering Circular by sending you a notice within two business days after the completion of our sale to you that contains the URL where the continued support, and I hope each of you are doing well during these continued crazy times.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eIn our last update, I started giving more color to our \u003ca href=\"http://www.applaudable.com\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eApplaudable\u003c/a\u003e platform. The results we previously saw through early user testing had been promising. They’re even better today. While the platform is very much still in beta, we have been continuing our user acquisition testing and the results have honestly been astounding, with a ~50% enrollment rate among invited users. We believe we can achieve user growth in line or beyond that of the most prominent social platforms of our times, projecting 3x faster growth during our pre-launch than Clubhouse experienced in the 6 months after their launch. With no revenue, their user growth reportedly led to a $4 billion valuation in less than 2 years.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eGiven the significance our expected user growth would have on our valuation, we have slow-rolled our Series A, planning on launching it simultaneous with Applaudable being released from beta. We expect we will open the round in September. As I’ve stated before, we are obligated to make $2 million of the raise available at a $36 million valuation, however beyond this early allocation, we believe there may be basis for increasing this substantially and we will look closely at whether to shut down the offering once the initial $2 million is raised, and subsequently relaunch it at a higher valuation.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eMoro and Applaudable have a symbiotic relationship, sharing user attention, content, and data. For the foreseeable future, this will be the nature of the relationship between the two. When the Advertiser and Affiliate programs are enabled for Applaudable in the future, we expect a tighter integration whereby the home furnishings category on Applaudable will be captured by the robust Moro marketplace we continue to develop. \u0026nbsp;We believe dozens of other verticals – fashion, food, electronics, fitness, jewelry, etc. - \u0026nbsp;can be monetized on Applaudable through advertisers and affiliates.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eWe are starting to give many of you access to register and reserve your usernames. Please jump on that! And we look forward to getting you on the platform as soon as possible.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eWe are still not accepting early investments but will keep you updated on when that may start and are doing our best to ensure that existing investors will have access to the round. \u0026nbsp;Lastly, check out my \u003ca href=\"https://www.instagram.com/tv/CO-mjb3gTod/\" id=\"offerings_update_form_4442_offerings_update_body\"\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eDear Investors,\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eThere are some important developments we wanted to share operationally, as well as with the upcoming offering.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eAs we continue to develop Applaudable and expand user acquisition model testing, we are increasingly optimistic about our prospects of achieving substantial user growth. As suggested in my previous update, we believe user growth may surpass that of market-leading social media firms during their corresponding development stages, following our full-scale launch. We are also seeing exciting early results from our new partnership with the leading business process outsourcing firm Assivo, which we expect will expand our onboarded marketplace-products by 20x over the next 4-5 months, significantly growing revenue.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u0026nbsp;As you know, we have partnered with Republic to make our Series A round available through their platform at a $36 million valuation, seeking $7.5 mm in incremental capital. \u0026nbsp;(The round in total will be close to $10 mm when taking into account the conversion of existing convertible notes and SAFE agreements.) \u0026nbsp;We have committed to ensuring that the A round will be made available to all current investors. \u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eWe expect to be in a position to submit our offering documents to the SEC for approval as early as the end of April. \u0026nbsp;Nonetheless, we also believe it is prudent to raise a modest bridge round in advance of the Series A to ensure that we have substantial runway even in the event that there were an unexpected delay in going live.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eFurthermore, given current momentum, we expect to achieve revenue and user growth milestones which could justify a substantial increase to the contemplated Series A valuation. \u0026nbsp;Increasing our runway through the bridge round will give us flexibility should we determine that this expected growth is indeed being achieved. \u0026nbsp;If so, it could be in the best interests of Moro and our investors to launch the Series A later in the year, and at a higher valuation.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u0026nbsp;Alternatively, after closing our bridge round, we could elect to go live with the Series A in the near term at the $36 million valuation, but closely assess whether we are “outgrowing” that valuation and suspend the offering before the full $7.5 million is raised, take a breath, and re-launch the offering at a higher valuation. \u0026nbsp;In either event, we will allow early investment reservations before the Series A is live, which will be the only period during which we will ensure its availability to our existing investors at the opening valuation.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u0026nbsp;We will make the bridge offering available on a very limited basis this month and will keep you closely apprised as to the timing of the $7.5mm Series A go-live. \u0026nbsp;We thank you for your support and interest and look forward to providing more information and updates shortly.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e name=\"offerings_update[body]\" id=\"offerings_update_form_4360_offerings_update_body\"\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eI want to start by expressing gratitude and excitement to you for your continued support as we navigated through an unrecognizable year relative to that on which we based our plans. We’re thrilled about the progress we’ve made and I wanted to share some of the highlights.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eBoth in terms of conserving cash to extend our runway and adjusting strategic initiatives based on changes to the market and its needs, we’ve responded quickly and decisively to the pandemic. I've previously shared that we'd be prioritizing Marketplace Growth and User Stories. The progress we've made against both over the last year was dramatically accelerated and may have taken 5x as long under normal circumstances. We couldn't be more excited about the progress and what the future holds.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eBefore sharing updates on these two initiatives, I want to announce some exciting changes to our team. The team size is more than 30 today, and we've made several notable additions which we're incredibly excited about. Laurence Levi, a founder of consumer private equity firm VO2 Partners and formerly the Chief Strategy Officer of Flowr Corp (TSXV: FLWR) \u0026nbsp;is now our Executive Chairman and has been working closely with me on key strategic initiatives, including leading our upcoming capital raise. Additionally, Claude Zoghzoghi, a Columbia Business School graduate with an MS in Business Analytics, has joined the team as our Director of Operations. He manages the company's overall operations, including hiring, partnerships, and systems \u0026amp; processes. And most recently, Stefanos Varotsis, who brings significant experience with digital brand marketing, has joined as Director of Digital and is managing and overseeing all of our Digital Marketing \u0026amp; User Acquisitions programs.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eMarketplace\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eGiven that we expect the vast majority of our marketplace revenue to be driven by design professionals, unlike consumer-focused home goods marketplaces, we focused on expanding the marketplace product offering itself rather than on our target market, which was largely on hold. In doing so, we have expanded brand acquisition to the tune of over 2 million products. As of today, only approximately 5% of those two million+ products are populated in our marketplace. This 5% is driving $1.5 million in annualized revenue. This week, we have finalized a partnership with a leading BPO firm that will manage the onboarding of the remaining 95% of products over the next ~ six months, effectively growing the size of our product offering by 20x. While we believe that revenue can grow in proportion to that expansion, we are conservatively projecting this will have a 10x direct revenue impact. With an end-to-end system in place for acquiring sellers and populating our marketplace with their products, we plan to expand the marketplace an additional 3x to more than 7 million products by 2023.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eUser Stories\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eThe results we have seen here have been exceptional. We have developed a proprietary user acquisition model that leveraged the systems we have used to build our community of design professionals to acquire end-users who would also share photos of their homes. We handled the early testing through the Moro brand. The results suggested that the success in terms of user acquisition and the content/experiences these users shared were not limited to those interested in sharing photos of inspiring interiors. These results were significant enough to warrant acceleration of an expanded version of the platform to include a broader range of users and content/experiences. We branded this \u003ca href=\"http://applaudable.com/\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eApplaudable\u003c/a\u003e and developed its early stages in beta. The platform enables users to share meaningful personal experiences that can inspire and inform improvements in others' lives, much like photos of homes have been doing on Moro since our inception. This is something we are increasingly excited about.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eEarly indications suggest that Applaudable could outpace Instagram and Clubhouse’s user growth over the first few months. We have delayed the opening of our Series A such that the early parts of the round will coincide with the expected start of that user growth. Such growth can substantially impact our valuation, and we reserve the right to amend the terms of the deal to reflect the increase in value. At such a point, we may also increase the minimum investment threshold. We will make an early allocation of our upcoming offering available at a minimum investment amount of $1,000. This minimum and the $36 million opening valuation are only guaranteed early in the offering for an undetermined time period.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eFor the time being, we are not accepting early investments; however, we are doing our best to ensure that existing investors will have access to the early allocation once we open it up. We expect to submit the offering circular for SEC review within 2 weeks and will follow up with further information shortly about participating in the offering.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eBest Regards,\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eAndrew Christodoulides\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eFounder \u0026amp; CEO\u003ca href=\"http://moro.com\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003e\u003cimg src=\"https://uploads.republic.co/p/images/attachments/original/000/024/287/24287-1598284247-45a7e81a00d81b867c2de0d6f7ad2302ba3cedce.png\" class=\"fr-fic fr-dib fr-fil\" style=\"width: 62px;\"\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eDear Investor:\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eIt goes without saying that these are extraordinary times. Our hearts continue to go out to everyone affected by COVID-19 and our gratitude to all of our stakeholders who have continued to show unrelenting support as we navigate through this. As always, our first responsibility during these uncertain times is the health and safety of our employees and the company's financial security. At the onset of the crisis, Moro took action to enable our employees to work from home as we closed our offices.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eWith all of our sellers, domestic and abroad, unable to fulfill orders at different times, and on the market side, with the designers and architects forced to put their projects on hold, like many businesses, we had to adjust our short-term strategy quickly. We were decisive in our actions to ensure survival, even in the worst of scenarios. We cut operating costs, delayed the relaunch, and minimized reliance on cashflow from an uncertain market. But we wanted to achieve far more than just coming out of these difficult times on our own two feet. We looked at how to best serve our stakeholders based on the new market conditions. Two of the developments we're most excited about are:\u003c/p\u003e\u003col\u003e\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eSubstantial Marketplace Growth\u003c/strong\u003e - With in-store retail taking one of the hardest hits during the pandemic, sellers needed new channels for exposure and distribution. Accordingly, we focused resources on accelerating seller acquisition. In total, over four hundred of the best category-brands have joined Moro, growing our marketplace product-offering to levels rivaling the leading competing platforms.\u003c/li\u003e\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eUser Stories\u003c/strong\u003e - Recognizing that most of us have changed the way we live, limiting our physical interactions and spending far more time in our homes (which often double as our offices), we looked for meaningful ways to foster connection and improve our experiences. Through public profiles and stories, we're opening up the platform for individual users to share their environments and their experiences within them, furthering our mission to inspire and inform improvements in others' lives. Based on early testing, we project this initiative will drive millions of end-users to the platform.\u003c/li\u003e\u003c/ol\u003e\u003cp\u003eWhile the challenges brought on by the global pandemic are still before us, the activities on which we rely, on both on the market and supply side, to drive revenue, are opening back up. Accordingly, we believed this was the appropriate time to launch Moro and recommence our user growth initiatives.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eWe couldn't be more excited. And while User Stories will initially only be available by invitation, we'd love to include some of you. Please visit \u003ca href=\"http://moro.com\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003emoro.com\u003c/a\u003e to register using the email address where you received this letter, and we'll share an invitation link.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eFor our official Moro launch announcement, please visit \u003ca href=\"https://www.instagram.com/p/CERpbXdpufh/?utm_source=ig_web_copy_link\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003ehttps://www.instagram.com/p/CERpbXdpufh/?utm_source=ig_web_copy_link\u003c/a\u003e\u003cem\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/em\u003e\u003cem\u003eF\u003c/em\u003eollow \u003ca href=\"https://www.instagram.com/morocommunity/\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003ehttps://www.instagram.com/morocommunity/\u003c/a\u003e while you're there for access to some of the best in design: spaces, objects, and living.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eThe Moro launch will mark the recommencement of user acquisition initiatives, and the launch of the ~400 (and growing) marketplace sellers. We're getting off to a delayed start with a market still in turmoil, however, we see relatively substantial opportunities to grow revenues. We will drive primary revenue through our marketplace model while simultaneously experimenting with advertising and affiliate models, which may enable us to more broadly monetize the massive amounts of content being published to Moro.com.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eThanks to our fiscal discipline, we have great liquidity and access to capital, when and if we need it. It's also important to remember that we have grown key components of Moro, including marketplace and content to levels comparable to the leading platforms in the space, some of which are valued in the multi-billions. Again, these are unpredictable times, and the extent of the pandemic's economic impact is still uncertain. But with the precautions we are taking, our investments in technologies adjusted for current market conditions, special accommodations for our customers, and our continued fiscal discipline, I believe Moro is well-positioned to serve all stakeholders during this critical time – and well into the future. When this crisis has abated, I believe that we will emerge in an even better position to lead the category with image-based search and shopping.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eLastly, I also want to acknowledge that many of you have reached out directly, and I apologize for not being able to respond individually. In addition to how we have dealt with the pandemic and general progress we've made, which I've addressed through the two letters I've shared, many of you have been asking about our Series A. While still not final, we intend to allocate a piece of the next round, to existing stakeholders. Details will be provided as we firm things up over the next couple of months.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eIf you have any questions that I have not addressed here, please feel free to reach out, and I will do my best to respond as quickly as possible.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eI am looking forward to sharing our results with you at the end of our first complete quarter later this year. Thank you for your continued support of Moro.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eSincerely,\u0026lt;br\u0026gt;Andrew Christodoulides\u0026lt;br\u0026gt;Founder and CEO\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cimg id=\"offerings_update_form_1975_edit_offerings_update_1975\" action=\"/offerings/updates/welivv-andrew-christodoulides-welivv-covid-19-update-i-hope-you-and-39-re-all\" accept-charset=\"UTF-8\" /\u003e\u003cinput type=\"hidden\" name=\"authenticity_token\" value=\"qbO4x8eU7qJgJO6XhXOa_EZjPukx-gSVFmAfp1me0aJZVk_E1x5ZOTKRE-OsYAgvCXWJYKzJiNZY0jhPYVkM8w\" autocomplete=\"off\" /\u003e\u003cdiv class=\"c-input-group\"\u003e\u003clabel class=\"c-label\" for=\"offerings_update_form_1975_offerings_update_headline\"\u003eTitle\u003c/label\u003e\u003cinput required=\"required\" class=\"c-input name=\"offerings_update[headline]\" id=\"offerings_update_form_1975_offerings_update_headline\" /\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cdiv class='js-headline_error u-colorRed s-marginPullTop0_5 s-marginBottom1 s-marginLeft0_5 u-hidden'\u003e\nEntry must be under 140 characters.\n\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cdiv class='offerings-updates-shared-form__body-wrapper'\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"c-input-group\"\u003e\u003ctextarea class=\"c-input--textarea c-input c-input--required js-offering_update_body\" required=\"required\" data-show-video-autoplay-button=\"false\" data-file-formats=\"application/msword application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document application/pdf application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.presentationml.presentation\" aria-required=\"true\" placeholder=\"Description\" name=\"offerings_update[body]\" id=\"offerings_update_form_1975_offerings_update_body\"\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eI hope you're all safe, healthy, and managing during these difficult times. Our world, as we knew it, at least for now, has changed, affecting us all in our personal and business capacities. Now, more than ever, we need to be our strongest, smartest, and best selves. People all over the world are feeling the economic effects of this crisis, and unfortunately, this is likely only the beginning. None of us chose this, but we do get to choose how we respond to it. Albeit difficult, I believe we have an opportunity and a responsibility here to one another, and ourselves. We must adapt in proportion to the challenges. If we do, we will not only survive, but I believe we will come out of it much better than we went in.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eWith much of Europe and the US being on lockdown, WeLivv revenue is at a standstill for the time being. We expect that this may be the case for another month or two, and even then, we don't expect a semblance of the old normal until months later. We are making adjustments to our plans, priorities, and operating strategies, based on a projected 80% average market drop-off for 12-18 months. We recognize that this may be overcompensating, and if things end up not being as dire, or getting back on track sooner, we too will recalibrate. During the current uncertainty, we will err on the side of caution.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eWe consider ourselves relatively lucky. Not only are we among a segment of businesses - purely digital, home-focused - that stands to ultimately benefit from the current situation and even more from the changes we expect may remain long after Covid-19 passes, but we have some unique opportunities based on our specific model and the stage of our company that can catalyze us. Our mission has always aimed to improve how people live. We're focused on doing this through the spaces we inhabit, the objects we surround ourselves with, and the human connections we foster. In my view, there has never been a greater need for this in our lifetime.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eAs you already know, the basis for us delivering on this mission starts with identifying and curating photos of great homes. We do this through the most celebrated designers and architects throughout the world. We also aim to make the products featured in the photos of homes being published to our platform available for purchase through our marketplace. Collectively, these are the primary drivers of platform growth and revenue. In both cases, for supply (the brands/sellers producing/selling products) and demand (the designers, architects, etc.), recognition and exposure are the primary motivators for them joining our platform. They, like almost all other businesses, are suffering during these difficult times. To compensate for the losses they're enduring, they are more interested than ever in joining the WeLivv platform. This is resulting in record-high enrollments and onboarding rates on the user and seller sides. To offset the 80% average market drop-off we're expecting, we're setting user and marketplace growth objectives of 5x our original targets. To this end, and to ensure we stay on solid financial ground, short-term, we're reallocating the majority of resources to supporting this aggressive growth and offsetting the projected revenue impact caused by this global pandemic.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eI'd also like to share some of the other milestones we reached and what's ahead outside of platform growth and revenue objectives. Although only released in beta, we have developed v1 of our Visual Discovery functionality. We also secured MORO (Moro.com) as the new brand name, and we're working on intellectual property protections. Our team is also growing, now including a computer science Ph.D. While the vast majority of resources, for the time being, will be concentrated on maximizing growth, we're also continuing to invest in product development/engineering. We'll be releasing the new UX, including visual discovery functionality and the rebrand, in May. Because we've shifted engineering resources away from longer-term product development initiatives, we'll be releasing many advancements and features. These are primarily focused on and around our current growth priorities, as well as on enabling sharing and fostering connections in ways that can help users through these difficult times.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eFrom a resource standpoint, we're in great shape today, however, we will likely delay our Series A. The investment group - which includes founders/leadership from Facebook, Stripe, Snapchat, Warby Parker and some of the other amazing companies of our generation - which we intended to give our deal to, is still interested. Despite their continued interest, however, we may delay opening the round for now out of concern that market conditions may ultimately play a role. We believe it may be prudent to wait until conditions are such that we are more confident we will close the round at the valuation and terms we expect. We have, however, allowed several existing investors to increase their investment, giving them access to our original capital raise terms, despite WeLivv being significantly further along. To further increase our financial buffer amid Series A delays, we are still allowing this for now. Reach out to me directly about this if you're interested.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eWhile the year has certainly not begun the way any of us expected, for reasons beyond any of our control, again, we \u003cstrong\u003eare\u003c/strong\u003e in complete control of \u003cstrong\u003ehow\u003c/strong\u003e we respond. WeLivv is in a unique position to not only get through these difficult times but, in doing so, come out of this closing the gap with today's market leaders significantly faster than we initially expected. I challenge you to look for those opportunities for yourself, as well.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eIn closing, I want to leave with you a question originally asked by Scott Galloway: \u003cem\u003eHow do each of us want to be remembered, years after this crisis? Near all of us will talk about this in grand terms one day. This is historic. What will be each of our roles in this moment of history?\u003c/em\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eI urge you to think about this in the context of your personal and professional lives. How can you grow through this? How can you play a role in the life of someone else?\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eStay vigilant, positive, and strong.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eAndrew\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003c/textarea\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cdiv class='s-marginTop1_5 id=\"offerings_update_form_1635_offerings_update_body\"\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eDear Investor:\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eThis is the first investor update, which includes original and new (i.e., Republic investors), so I apologize in advance for any redundant information which may have previously been communicated.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eWe've made immense progress over the last half-year and have started 2020 with significant momentum. I want to update you on key developments, as well as on what's ahead.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eIn May 2019, we decided to prioritize the integration of our CTO, key patents, and our rebrand, over our Series A. While we had achieved the validation - user, product, and revenue - we were aiming for by early 2019, our board strongly felt that we should get these elements of the business organized before publicly opening up an institutional round. To this end, we opened a $1 million bridge through Republic, which ended up oversubscribed when we closed it in December.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eIn addition to the successful round, which drew in a great new network of investors, during this same time, we brought on an amazing CTO and begun the patent process. The rebrand is still in the works but has moved slower than planned based on trademark risks associated with the new name. We also expanded the engineering team and began building a merchandising team, which includes two industry veterans. Lastly, we have continued to develop and refine our computer vision model, as well as user growth, and marketplace expansion, collectively the primary drivers of revenue.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eWe're in the best position we've ever been in, and we expect the momentum to continue, driving considerable revenue growth throughout the year. We've also substantially accelerated product development. The first version of our Visual Discovery tool (allowing searching of on-platform images) has been developed and tested. We planned on releasing it January but later decided that UI changes to the existing platform were warranted in order to provide a more seamless user experience. It is planned for release late this month with 250k photos.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eMore than ever, we believe we're on track to build something special - something that differentiates in quality from the leading relevant platforms, including Pinterest and Houzz, and in doing so, scale the company making it an attractive acquisition target. Our Series A will be used to catalyze product development and revenue growth. In addition to traditional venture capital, we're actively entertaining several options for raising our first priced round. We're very well positioned to manage current product, operating, and growth objectives, and are not in need of additional capital to do so, however, we've made a strategic decision to allow a new angel investor the option to invest based on the added value that he brings. We're doing so based on the same convertible note terms previously offered (24% discount, 12%PIK). Given he will have access to these same terms while the company is considerably further along in its development, in the interest of fairness, we are allowing you the same deal access. Given we are actively working on our Series A, this is something we will only allow through the end of February.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eWe are near finalizing the closing of the round with Republic and will immediately work on getting the perks out as quickly as possible thereafter. We are grateful for the high level of continued support among our investor group and are excited about what's ahead.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eWarmest regards,\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003c/textarea\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cdiv class='s-marginTop1_5 s-marginPullHoriz1 s-paddingHoriz1 id=\"offerings_update_form_1466_offerings_update_body\"\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eAs we end the year, I find myself feeling immensely grateful for where things are for me personally, and with WeLivv. The amazing position the company is currently is not something I take for granted as it did not come easy. As many of you recently joined the WeLivv family, I wanted to share a personal story about the journey which I shared with my personal network as we were launching the Republic campaign several months ago.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e_______\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eThe last three years have been harder, more isolating, and more stressful than anyone will ever know. Today, I'm working toward building something greater than I could have ever dreamed at the start of it all, because of the difficulties, not despite them.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eMy 3rd startup journey has been so much more than I could have imagined. We started with this simple idea that we could do one small thing within digital commerce better than the norms at the time. Since the start of our development, the original idea has evolved into so much more, and the progress we made has been far greater than we originally imagined. With both, came many more challenges and uncertainties. I have been tested emotionally, mentally, and even physically in ways I didn't realize I could handle. There have been so many failures with real consequences. The magnitude of fears, insecurities, self-doubt, and even self-loathing that I felt over the years are hard to describe. Each time I made it through one of these bouts, I came out with even greater clarity, confidence, and energy. But it's a cycle that continues as boundaries are pushed. For me personally, there were \"easy ways out\" with attractive job opportunities along the way, but nothing was less comfortable or scarier than the thought of having to live with myself after giving up.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eThe unrelenting commitment and focus, in part, is just how I'm wired, but it's also magnified by a strong sense of obligation to everyone who has supported me in any way. In addition to the entire team all over the world that has been along for this ride and has forced themselves to go deeper, and work harder, to achieve more than they too probably realized they could, I have also had amazing support beyond them. I have had friends that supported me as if the opportunities and challenges I was dealing with were their own. I've had partners and investors who stepped up repeatedly to open doors, break nights brainstorming critical problems or decisions, and ensured we were supported in the direction we chose. And last but not least, I've had a family that has stayed supportive and confident in me - no matter how difficult things seemed, and no matter how radical the vision grew - even when I didn't feel it myself.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eThis journey has felt lonely much of the time, but the reality is I have not been alone. The people I've surrounded myself with deserve more credit than I do, for the position I'm in and for the magnitude of the opportunity that is taking shape through WeLivv.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eWe're now developing aspects of the business that challenge the status quo of online search from companies like Google, the norms for how we're accustomed to shopping online, as well as how we consume and interact with content through the very platform you're reading this on. We're crazy enough to believe we can do it better.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eWe've raised $1 million from friends, family, and industry/technology icons. We've attracted incredible talent into the company, including the founder and exCEO of one of the most successful startups of our generation. These are testaments to what we have achieved so far. And we haven't scratched the surface yet. We're at a point where critical aspects of what we're doing are no longer theoretical. We have developed and proven foundational elements. And while I'm not under any delusion that anything will now come easy, I do believe the times ahead will be even more exciting than those we've already seen.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eI've pushed through everything I've been dealt, and no matter how bad I felt or tough things got, saw every problem and opportunity for everything I would want it to be, rather than what it was. I've been pushing to see those things materialize through many ups and downs. That's why, as I sit in my little Queens apartment writing this, I have the audacity to work toward something so immense in its complexity and potential.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eThank you to everyone who helped in any way. You all know who you are. Every little bit of it played a role.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e_______\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eThanks again for sharing in the vision and for becoming a part of our story.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eSincerely, Andrew\u003c/p\u003e\u003c/textarea\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cdiv class='s-marginTop1_5 s-marginPullHoriz1 s-paddingHoriz1 This plan will essentially cover the period we need to position ourselves for our institutional Series A. We’ll also be reaching out directly to coordinate delivery and enablement of each of the perks associated with your level of investment in WeLivv.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eFor those of you who have been asking about reserving public @usernames, you will be receiving an email allowing you to do this before it becomes publicly available.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eFinally, we have received numerous messages about Series A participation. name=\"offerings_update[body]\" id=\"offerings_update_form_1400_offerings_update_body\"\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eAs our campaign is coming to a very successful close, we couldn’t be happier about what we’ve achieved in parallel, and the unique position we’re in, going into the new year. We’ve set ourselves up to scale key components of the business at levels that rival today’s market leaders including Pinterest and Houzz. I’d like to focus on the areas of focus where we expect to see the most significant growth throughout 2020:\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003ePRO USERS \u0026amp; PHOTOS\u003c/strong\u003e - Design and design-related professionals are at the heart of what we’re doing in multiple ways. They represent the largest segment of the market, accounting for over $100 billion/year in product spend. And, in our view, equally importantly, they are the source of the professionally photographed interiors projects which will be the basis for our Visual Discovery \u0026amp; Shopping platform. We expect to onboard more than 65,000 curated Design Professionals in 2020, putting us on par with Houzz (valued at $4+ billion) in terms of the size of our image library.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eIMAGE DATA\u003c/strong\u003e - In our estimation, more important than the quantity of images we’re projecting, is the actual quality of these images and the data accuracy of their contents. This is the single greatest differentiator between WeLivv, and today’s leading comparable platforms including Houzz, Pinterest, and even more broadly Google Images and Instagram. The curated, proprietary approach we’re taking to building our platform - which is in direct contrast to the broad, open approaches used by the aforementioned platforms - uniquely positions us to identify and tag products at the SKU-level, which will enable a more cohesive end-to-end user experience starting with Visual Discovery \u0026amp; Search, all the way through to allowing users to complete the on-platform purchase of exactly what they see. Again, while achieving scale comparable to today’s leading platform is very significant, we believe the difference in image and data quality, will prove to be the big winner for WeLivv and its stakeholders.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eMARKETPLACE\u003c/strong\u003e - Our revenue model is based entirely on the on-platform purchase of products which will be inventoried and fulfilled by their respective manufacturers and/or sellers. We have developed a merchandising strategy and related resources, to target the expansion of our existing marketplace by 500% to 200k+ total products by the end of 2020.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eEND USERS\u003c/strong\u003e - While our focus thus far has been on Pro Users, we have been working on broader marketing and user acquisition strategies which we will be rolling out early in the new year. As mentioned last week, this will include a) Network Effects strategies driven by the expansion of our featured design professionals and their respective audiences, the growth of our image library and the broad styles and preferences they will serve, as well as the expansion of our marketplace and the associated brands; b) Content which will largely be driven by the amazing projects being published to WeLivv on a daily basis, as well as the products being offered through our marketplace; and c) a proprietary direct user acquisition model which we expect will enable us to drive end users to the platform at a ratio of 2:1 over professional users, by the end of 2020.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eREVENUE\u003c/strong\u003e - We are targeting gross revenue of $2 million per month, by December 2020 which would be a 1400% increase over our peak gross monthly revenue in 2019.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eWe are very excited about the progress we’ve made, enabling us to go into the new year with such a massive opportunity, and great clarity in the path to watching it come to fruition. As I write this email, I also want to acknowledge the immense appreciation I have for everyone - our amazing team, each and every one of our investors, and our many partners - who has helped make this possible. I am truly grateful for each and every one of you and I feel a deep sense of obligation to deliver on the vision I’ve laid out. I’m lucky to have this opportunity.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eThank you to each of you.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eAndrew Christodoulides, Co-Founder \u0026amp; Plus, an ask. \" name=\"offerings_update[headline]\" id=\"offerings_update_form_1375_offerings_update_headline\" /\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cdiv class='js-headline_error u-colorRed s-marginPullTop0_5 s-marginBottom1 s-marginLeft0_5 u-hidden'\u003e\nEntry must be under 140 characters.\n\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cdiv class='offerings-updates-shared-form__body-wrapper'\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"c-input-group\"\u003e\u003ctextarea class=\"c-input--textarea c-input c-input--required js-offering_update_body\" required=\"required\" data-show-video-autoplay-button=\"false\" data-file-formats=\"application/msword application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document application/pdf application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.presentationml.presentation\" aria-required=\"true\" placeholder=\"Description\" name=\"offerings_update[body]\" id=\"offerings_update_form_1375_offerings_update_body\"\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eWe’ve been firing on all cylinders like never before. I’ve recently shared updates on the progress we’ve made with user growth and marketplace expansion as we prepare to launch coordinated full-scale initiatives toward both of these objectives, which collectively serve as the greatest impact on revenue. As the year is coming to a close and we prepare for the exciting things we believe to be ahead for 2020, we wanted to share updates on two other important elements of the platform:\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eProduct Development -\u0026nbsp;\u003c/strong\u003eOur top engineering priority has been the development of our Visual Discovery Tool (i.e., Contextualized/Image-based search experience), and we’re pleased to confirm that we plan to release this on January 20, 2020. The initial release of this User Experience will focus on the visual search and discovery of millions of curated interior design images based on users’ styles and preferences, for the purposes of matching users with professionals. This initial version will be the basis for the ultimate vision of the platform, while also helping shape future iterations based on analysis of user behavior. Further, the initial functionality is expected to indirectly contribute to the growth of our professional user community based on the increased brand awareness afforded to design professionals through this new search channel.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eEnd Users -\u0026nbsp;\u003c/strong\u003eWhile our core user focus centers on the professional design community, we have been diligently working on a substantially broader user acquisition strategy. The three critical components of the strategy are a) Network Effects, b) Content, and c) Direct. Our primary focus and what I’m most excited to report on is our direct model. We’ve developed a strategy that largely mimics the approach we’ve developed and proven for acquiring professional users, and we’ve seen equally astounding acquisition rates through the testing we’ve done. Unlike professional users who typically have websites featuring their work, which we are able to assess for fit, end-users cannot be identified and qualified in the same manner. To compensate for this, we’re analyzing behaviors via social media channels, including Instagram, Facebook, and Pinterest. Through this approach, we are able to identify relevant users based on the content they distribute or engage with, as well as the accounts in which they follow. For the purposes of this update, I will focus on two key facts: 1) the potential audience is many times bigger than that of the professional users we’ve been focused on to date, and the market is as much as 4x greater as well; 2) we are acquiring these users through a direct approach at a rate of 65%. We expect this to represent a substantial portion of acquired end-users, with relatively low acquisition costs given no 3rd party/paid component.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eWe’re obviously very excited about these developments!\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eIn addition to this, I wanted to take this opportunity to ask for some help. While we’re still finalizing our HR plan for next year, there are a few roles we are looking to fill in the near future. If you know anyone that you think would be an absolutely amazing fit for any of the following roles (they do not need to be based in NYC), we’d appreciate the referrals (please send to \u003ca href=\"mailto:hr@welivv.com\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003ehr@welivv.com\u003c/a\u003e):\u003c/p\u003e\u003cul\u003e\u003cli\u003eSenior Python Engineer\u003c/li\u003e\u003cli\u003eMarketing Director (should have marketing management experience working in home or luxury domain)\u0026nbsp;\u003c/li\u003e\u003cli\u003eSocial Media Manager (must have experience working on micro-influencer campaigns)\u003c/li\u003e\u003cli\u003eOperations Manager (must have 3+ years experience working with high-growth consumer-facing technology platform)\u0026nbsp;\u003c/li\u003e\u003c/ul\u003e\u003cp\u003eAs always, we’re grateful for the continued support we receive from our investor group, and we’re really excited about what’s ahead.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eBest, Andrew\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003c/textarea\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cdiv class='s-marginTop1_5 s-marginPullHoriz1
