encompassing users, images, and marketplace products, we have begun stress-testing our systems, vetting our data, and generally making sure our t’s are crossed, and i’s dotted. The immediate results we’re seeing are INCREDIBLE! We expanded our professional user base by more than 20% to over 10k since yesterday! These results are not just reassuring to our user growth plans for 2020 and beyond, but should they continue, will likely result in the upward revision of our user growth projections significantly beyond the 65k we previously projected for next year. We’re even seeing professional users we’ve onboarded to WeLivv, begin to invest in WeLivv through \u003ca href=\"http://republic.co/welivv\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003erepublic.co/welivv\u003c/a\u003e. Having users with a vested interest in the company is something we see as extremely valuable, and so this is something we will make a more concerted effort to increase over the final few weeks of the offering.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eWhile US-based designers, decorators, and architects have been our primary focus up until now, we decided to include other verticals and markets in our recent user acquisition tests. The results we saw from lighting designers, home stagers, home-builders, and home contractors, as well as from international markets including Brazil, France, UK, and Japan, have been very much in line with prior metrics. This is incredibly exciting as it indicates that we will be able to broaden our acquisition efforts while maintaining comparably high results.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eFinally, we believe the proprietary systems we’ve developed are running as intended and will facilitate efficient scaling, which we will methodically increase over the coming weeks with a plan to run at 100% operating (systems, personnel, etc.) in January. Based on the results we’ve seen, we are aiming to update our model and share plans and projections with you by the end of this month.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eWe’re pumped for what 2020 will bring and are happy to have you along for the ride!\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eAndrew\u003c/p\u003e\u003c/textarea\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cdiv class='s-marginTop1_5 s-marginPullHoriz1 name=\"offerings_update[body]\" id=\"offerings_update_form_1339_offerings_update_body\"\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eAs we’ve reported on throughout our Republic campaign, we haven’t been wasting any time pushing forward on priorities aimed at positioning us to scale and preparing us for our institutional Series A. We’re excited and grateful to be able to update you on one of the most critical elements of the platform: users. While, in one form or another, users are a vital part of any successful platform, in our case, design-related users play two very significant roles. First, they are the source of the millions of professionally photographed interiors that are central to our model. And second, they represent our largest customer segment accounting for over 25% of the total $500 billion home goods market.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eRecognizing the mutual value, we have worked diligently at establishing data, systems, and processes that will allow us to scale this portion of our user base - and as a byproduct, our image library and revenue - as rapidly as possible. We have developed and tested systems which we expect will allow us to onboard 65,000 curated, design, and home professionals by the end of 2020. We estimate that these users account for over $6 billion per month in product purchases. Further, based on current averages of 450 images per onboarded design professional, we expect to surpass today’s leading platform in terms of images by nearly 2x. The projected growth of our professional user base, in conjunction with previously reported plans to rapidly expand our product marketplace, is expected to drive gross monthly revenue of over $2 million per month, by the end of 2020, while at the same time this is barely scratching the surface relative to the total revenue opportunity represented by this market.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eWhile the revenue opportunity is significant and is expected to facilitate other key objectives including attractive Series A terms, the data we’re amassing directly and indirectly through such rapid growth of users and content, is strengthening our unique ability to enable visual (i.e., image-based) search and discovery, and shopping, in ways the today’s leading search, social, and shopping platforms, have not yet achieved. As previously mentioned, we are working toward the first release of this visual discovery user experience on January 20, 2020. \u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eWe’re excited about the progress we’re making and the wind at our backs as we get ready to scale into 2020. As always, we’re grateful for the continued support we receive from our investor group.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eVery Best, id=\"offerings_update_form_1296_offerings_update_body\"\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eWithout us pursuing Venture Capital, there has been a natural uptick in VC interest in WeLivv. Some of this interest has led to formal meetings earlier than we had planned on commencing the process of pursuing institutional investor opportunities. While this is very much still up in the air, based on the interest we're receiving, we're considering opportunities that could fill the remaining 50% of our $1 million target, effectively resulting in the early closing of the Republic-offering.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eOn the operations side, we have been focused on validating and developing resources for one of our primary 2020 priorities. We have performed a marketplace expansion program test which is driven by and integrated with our computer vision system, and have seen very promising results. To manage the scaling of this, we are entering into an agreement with two industry veterans. We expect to launch a full-scale merchandising program push in January, and in conjunction with a proportionate user acquisition effort through our proprietary model, believe we can grow revenue to $2 million per month by the end of 2020.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eLet me unpack it a bit. As you know, at the core of our ultimate visual search, discovery, and shopping user experience is our ability to not only amass millions of curated interior design images but, more importantly, our unique ability to identify and tag the contents of these images at a SKU level. This is what sets us apart from competing image-based search platforms, including Google, Pinterest, Houzz, and others. Comprehensive, accurate image tagging enables a rich, efficient, visual discovery (i.e., image-based refinable search) and shopping experience, the likes of which are not being offered by any other known platform today. Especially given the absence of recognizable brand names in the home-goods category, consumers are able to better understand and connect to products when seeing them in their authentic contexts via curated, professionally photographed interiors, rather than in the generic studio photography which is the norm on all search platforms and e-commerce sites today. The micro effects of this are that consumers who would not have otherwise been able to decide on the purchase of a high-ticket aesthetic item purchase before, now can. The macro effect is that we are able to increase online market penetration for the category in general.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eWhile this leads to a significant opportunity to set ourselves apart, positioning us to dominate a major industry, our various stakeholders are stand to benefit in direct proportion to us. While many of the manufacturers in the home-goods space, especially on the mid to higher-end - out of fear of cannibalizing their existing businesses - have very restrictive online dealer programs, they clearly see the value opportunity through the WeLivv platform. We put this to the test. We selected 20 manufacturers who produce products that are frequently being identified and tagged in photos that have been published to WeLivv, and we used these images to pique their interest in selling through the platform. Recognizing the exposure that they can receive through WeLivv, as well as the opportunity to capture untapped segments of the market, all 20 of them have signed up to our marketplace as sellers.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eWe are confident that this approach can be scaled across the millions of products (represented by thousands of manufacturers) that are being shown across the millions of images on our platform. We are entering into an arrangement to manage this massive merchandising growth initiative, not just in terms of brand acquisition, but also all of the peripheral elements, including content management, traffic generation, and image mapping. The growth of our product marketplace in conjunction with the expansion of our image library and respective image tagging, is a significant component of the ultimate vision of our platform as it facilitates both more efficient product tagging, but is also necessary for an end-top-end user experience whereby users can purchase all items that they discover, right on platform. In the interim, the growth of our marketplace is one of the two primary factors of revenue. We believe we will expand our product marketplace to over 1 million products by the end of 2020, enabling us to generate over $2 million per month in gross monthly revenue, among the 65,000 design professionals we also expect to onboard by that time.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eLook out for additional updates on the VC possibilities before us, as well as the implementation and scheduled full-scale launch of our marketplace initiatives, which will coincide with the launch of our user acquisition efforts, as well. As always, thank you for your continued support.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eBest, This is where we believe we can differentiate ourselves, not just from leading competing platforms such as Houzz and Pinterest, but from the norms in online search and shopping, in general. id=\"offerings_update_form_1226_offerings_update_body\"\u003e\n\u003cp\u003e\u003cspan\u003eThis week, I wanted to focus specifically on our top priorities related to positioning the company for its institutional priced venture round. In addition to the continued growth of our platform, the primary use of proceeds through the Republic offering are being allocated to IP Protection through patents, placement of several key roles, as well as our rebrand. We have begun working toward each of these priorities to allow us to move from the closing of the current offering to the commencement of our institutional round - which will enable scaling of the platform – as efficiently as possible.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cspan\u003eWe believe the propriety systems and software we're developing - central to the innovative user experience, as well as the magnitude of the opportunity - should be protected before being exposed to institutional investors. Working with company counsel at Dentons, we have begun the process of patenting key components of the software.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cspan\u003eAs I reported last week, we have finalized the selection of our CTO, and couldn't be more excited about where we landed. We have also been considering many incredible possibilities for Operations, Finance, Merchandising, and Marketing. We're narrowing down candidates, positioning ourselves to finalize selections, at the right time, based on our progress. We expect to announce our heads of Marketing and Merchandising within one month.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cspan\u003eRegarding the rebrand, I'd like to thank all of you who participated in the poll. Your feedback was very helpful, and it was great to have you involved in the process. The final names selected, in part based on the poll, were Lyvv, and Moro. Unfortunately, we hit a bit of a snag as there are potential trademark conflicts with both of these brand names. While this is something we are exploring further, we're cognizant of the fact that we may need to go back to the drawing board for the right brand name.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cspan\u003eLastly, we are overwhelmed and humbled by the amazing messages and support we have been receiving from many of you. It is quite exciting to have expanded our network through this offering in such a meaningful way. Many of you are leaders in related sectors, including design, marketing, finance, media, furniture, and technology and we place huge value in your perspectives, experiences, and networks.
